        SELECT [r].[Id], [r].[CreatedAt], [r].[CreatedBy], [r].[DeletedAt], [r].[DeletedBy], [r].[Description], [r].[IsActive], [r].[IsDeleted], [r].[IsSystem], [r].[Name], [r].[UpdatedAt], [r].[UpdatedBy]
        FROM [Roles] AS [r]
        WHERE [r].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [r0] ON [u0].[RoleId] = [r0].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [r1].[Id], [r1].[CreatedAt], [r1].[CreatedBy], [r1].[DeletedAt], [r1].[DeletedBy], [r1].[IsActive], [r1].[IsDeleted], [r1].[PermissionId], [r1].[RoleId], [r1].[UpdatedAt], [r1].[UpdatedBy], [p0].[Id] AS [Id0], [p0].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [p0].[CreatedBy] AS [CreatedBy0], [p0].[DeletedAt] AS [DeletedAt0], [p0].[DeletedBy] AS [DeletedBy0], [p0].[Description], [p0].[IsActive] AS [IsActive0], [p0].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted0], [p0].[Name], [p0].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [p0].[UpdatedBy] AS [UpdatedBy0]
        FROM [RolePermissions] AS [r1]
        INNER JOIN (
            SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[CreatedBy], [p].[DeletedAt], [p].[DeletedBy], [p].[Description], [p].[IsActive], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[UpdatedBy]
            FROM [Permissions] AS [p]
            WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
        ) AS [p0] ON [r1].[PermissionId] = [p0].[Id]
        WHERE [r1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [s] ON [r0].[Id] = [s].[RoleId]
    WHERE [u0].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [s0] ON [u1].[Id] = [s0].[UserId]
ORDER BY [u1].[Id], [s0].[Id], [s0].[Id0], [s0].[Id1]
